Could define param harmonic:

def make_harmonic(n, amp, freqs, phases, bias=1.0, seed?):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   f=bias
   for m, A, phi: f += A*(np.sin(2π m x)+phi?)
   f=np.maximum(f,0)
   normalize.
```
Instead of fixed shapes, we can use random perturb around uniform in low frequencies:
```
def make_lowfreq(n, seed, max_m):
   rng; f=np.ones(n); for m=1..max_m: c=rng.normal(0, sigma/sqrt(m)); phase; f += c sin(...)
   f=np.maximum(f, 0.001); normalize
```
This can produce diverse nonnegative shapes, maybe close to optimum.

Let's think of a more globally optimal algorithm: Because we want p* p flat, this is like making pair sum distribution uniform. We can use iterative "balancing" algorithm:
At each step, find a sum k with high b_k. To reduce it, move mass from indices i that are heavily involved in k to indices j that are less involved. This is like local search. Exact CD does that.

We can also use "greedy mass redistribution" over entire sequence: Set target for each b_k maybe decreasing from max. For each high k, solve for d (perturbation) that reduces b_k while not increasing active peaks. This is a linear program over d (direction). Could solve via projected gradient on d:
- Let active set A (top peaks). We want d with mean zero, norm 1, minimize ∑_{k∈A} λ_k ∇b_k·d (first-order). This is - maximize linear objective over d in tangent space and maybe nonneg constraints. Without norm constraint, solution d_i proportional to -∑ λ ∇_i (shifted) truncated? Mirror step does exp(-η grad). We can improve by choosing step size to maximize decrease estimated by quadratic: For active peaks, b_k(a+ηd)≈b_k+2η∇d+η²d^T Q_k d. The mirror descent step d = -a grad? On manifold multiplicative. Hard.

def mirror_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, start_step=0.1, max_active=80, temp_factor=0.2, anneal=True, verbose=False):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); best=a; best_m=max_conv(a); cur=a; cur_m=best_m; prev=a; t=0
    step=start_step; stall=0; restart=0
    while time_left:
       t+=1
       # Nesterov y
       beta = (t-1)/(t+2) if t>1 else 0
       y = a + beta*(a - prev)
       y = np.maximum(y, 0); sy=y.sum(); y=y/sy if sy>0 else a.copy()
       b,maxb=conv_max(y) # Need b for active and max.
       if maxb < cur_m: cur_m=maxb; if <best: update
       active, weights, gap = active_weights(b, maxb, temp_factor_current, max_active_current)
       grad=grad_from_active(y,b? active weights)
       max_abs=grad.max
       eta=step/max_abs if max_abs>0 else 0
       if eta==0: perturb
       v=np.log(y+1e-300) - eta*grad
       new=exp(v-v.max) ...
       # ensure nonneg sum
       new_m = max_conv(new)
       # acceptance
       tol = 1e-15
       if new_m < cur_m - tol:
           a=new; prev? a_prev=a_old? For Nesterov, set prev=a_old.
           cur_m=new_m; step=min(step*1.05,0.7); stall=0; best if
       elif anneal and new_m < cur_m + temp_accept:
           accept current (maybe); update a,prev,cur_m; step=...
           if cur_m > best_m: best unchanged
           stall=1
       else:
           step=max(step*0.7,0.005)
           # maybe small random perturbation to current? no
       # update temp_factor down over time, max_active up
       if stall > 2000: perturb
```

Need `compute_conv_max(a, fft_cache?)`: For repeated same a? We'll implement `fft_conv(a)` returning b and maxb.
```
def fft_conv(a):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; size=1<< (L-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)[:L]
   # numerical negative tiny: b=np.maximum(b,0)
   return b, b.max()
```
For active gradient based on y, we already have b. Good.

Now, let's reason about exact CD update formula and code correctness. We'll write a helper `move_lin_quad(a,b,i,j)`? Let's design to avoid repeated allocation of ks.

```
def compute_move_lin_quad(a, i, j):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    start=min(i,j); end=max(i,j)+n
    # end exclusive <=L? max i,j = n-1 -> end=2n-1? if n=5, n-1=4, +5=9, L=9; slice start:9 inclusive indices0..8. Good.
    length=end-start
    lin=np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
    quad=np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
    # source interval
    s_start=max(start, i); s_end=min(end, i+n)
    if s_start<s_end:
       ks = np.arange(s_start, s_end)
       mirror = ks - i
       lin[ks-start] -= 2.0*a[i]*a[mirror]
       k_self=2*i
       if s_start <= k_self < s_end:
           quad[k_self-start] += 1.0
    # dest
    d_start=max(start,j); d_end=min(end,j+n)
    if d_start<d_end:
       ks=np.arange(d_start,d_end)
       mirror=ks-j
       lin[ks-start] += 2.0*a[j]*a[mirror]
       k_self=2*j
       if d_start <= k_self < d_end:
           quad[k_self-start] += 1.0
    if i!=j:
       k=i+j
       if start <= k < end:
           quad[k-start] -= 2.0
    return start, end, lin, quad
```

def mirror_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, start_step=0.08, max_active=80, temp_factor=0.25, acceptance_rel=1e-4, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize(a0)
    n=len(a)
    b, cur_m = fft_conv(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=cur_m
    prev=a.copy()
    step=start_step
    tf=temp_factor
    ma=max_active
    accept_temp = acceptance_rel * max(cur_m, 1e-12)
    stall=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it +=1
       # Nesterov maybe
       if it == 1:
          y=a.copy()
       else:
          beta = 0.9 if it < 100 else 0.95? or (it-1)/(it+2)
          y = a + beta*(a - prev)
          # if y has negative large? clip
          ymin=np.min(y)
          if ymin < 0: y += (-ymin+1e-15)
          sy=y.sum()
          if sy<=0: y=a.copy()
          else: y/=sy
       b, cur_m_y = fft_conv(y)
       # maybe if cur_m_y < cur_m? update current? We are at y not a; accept new only after step. Could use y for gradient.
       maxb = b.max()
       # active weights
       minb = b.min()  # O(n)
       gap = maxb - minb
       if gap <= 1e-15:
           # flat
           break
       temp = tf * gap
       # active top: threshold = maxb - 0.5*gap? Use temp? For weights, we can just use top ma by threshold maxb - 0.5*gap.
       thr = maxb - max(1e-15, 0.5 * gap)
       active = np.where(b >= thr)[0]
       if active.size == 0:
           active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
       elif active.size > ma:
           # choose top ma
           part = np.argpartition(b, -ma)[-ma:]
           active = part
       active_b = b[active]
       w = np.exp((active_b - maxb) / temp)
       sw = w.sum()
       if sw <=0: w = np.ones_like(active_b); sw=len
       else: w /= sw
       grad = np.zeros(n)
       ai=y
       for wi,k in zip(w, active):
           k=int(k)
           lo = k - n + 1
           if lo < 0: lo=0
           hi = k
           if hi >= n: hi = n-1
           idx=np.arange(lo, hi+1)
           grad[idx] += 2.0 * wi * ai[k - idx]
       max_abs = np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if max_abs <= 1e-30:
           y = a + 1e-4*rng... maybe break
       eta = step / max_abs
       # cap eta? if eta > 1e4: eta=1e4
       logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
       v = logy - eta * grad
       vmax=v.max()
       new = np.exp(v - vmax)
       snew = new.sum()
       if snew <=0: new=y.copy()
       else: new /= snew
       new_m = fft_conv_max(new) # function just max
       # line search? if new_m > cur_m_y maybe no
       # We compare to current a's cur_m? We should accept if new_m < a_m (current best current) or anneal.
       # Maintain a_m = max of a. We have cur_m maybe a_m from last accepted. Need variable a_m.
```

Now, `make_lowfreq` must produce non-zero sum and normalized. Let's implement robust:
```
def make_lowfreq(n, seed=0, max_m=10, amp=0.7, floor=0.02):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    v = np.zeros(n)
    # include a monotone component maybe
    v += rng.normal(0,0.3)*np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for m in range(1, max_m+1):
       amp_m = amp/np.sqrt(m)
       coef = rng.normal(0, amp_m)
       phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
       v += coef * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
    f = 1.0 + v
    f = np.maximum(f, floor)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<1e-8: f=np.ones(n)*0.01+floor
    return f/s
```
But this floor biases; floor 0.02 may limit zeros. Could try floor=0.0 random. Use p=softplus: `f = floor + np.exp( v / scale )`? Simpler: `f = np.maximum(1.0 + v, 0.01)`. If v large negative, floor. Nonnegative. Normalization. This includes constant 1, so shape around uniform with zero-mean perturb. Good.

Could also use `make_exponential_skew`:
```
def make_skew(n, rate, wave_amp=0.5): x=linspace; f=np.exp(rate*x) * (1+wave_amp*np.sin(2πx)); return normalize(max(f,0))
```
But lowfreq random enough.

`normalize`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)/a.size if a.size else np.array([1.0])
    return a/s
```
If a is list.

`fft_conv`:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0: return np.array([0.0]), 0.0
    L = 2*n - 1
    size = 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=size)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=size)
    b = B[:L]
    # Clean negative tiny and maybe round? 
    if n < 2000:
       # exact? no
    neg = b < 0
    if np.any(neg): b[neg] = 0.0
    return b, float(b.max())
```
For very large arrays, `if np.any` scans; we already need max. Use `b = np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)` modifies. If tiny negative significant? ok.

`fft_conv_max` can call `fft_conv` and max; but that clips. We'll just use `fft_conv` everywhere to keep b if needed. In mirror candidate new, we don't need b but `fft_conv` returns. Overhead of returning b memory okay.

Could optimize by not clipping in max-only? Not worth.

Update best:
```
def update_best(tracker, a, label):
    if a is None: return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return
    try:
       sc = 2.0*len(a)*fft_conv(a)[1]/(s*s)
    except Exception:
       return
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if tracker['seq'] is None or sc < tracker['score'] - 1e-12:
       tracker['seq']=a; tracker['score']=sc; print(...)
```

Now, `mirror_search` code details with no closures.

Let's write fully in head:
```
def mirror_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, start_step=0.08, max_active=80, temp_factor=0.30, acceptance_rel=1e-4, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <=0: return a0
    a=normalize(a0)
    n=len(a)
    b, a_m = fft_conv(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=a_m
    best_score_fast = 2.0*n*best_m/(a.sum()**2) # sum1
    prev=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step=start_step
    accept_temp = acceptance_rel * best_m
    stall=0
    it=0
    restarts=0
    # parameters
    max_active_cur = min(30, max_active)
    temp_factor_cur = min(0.6, temp_factor)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        # adapt
        if it < 1:
           y=a.copy()
        else:
           # use momentum
           if it < 50:
               mu=0.5
           elif it < 500:
               mu=0.8
           else:
               mu=0.9
           y = a + mu * (a - prev)
           # if y has negative or bad, revert
           ymin = np.min(y)
           if ymin < 0.0:
               y = y + (-ymin + 1e-15)
           sy = y.sum()
           if sy <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(sy):
               y = a.copy()
           else:
               y = y / sy
        b, y_m = fft_conv(y)
        maxb = b.max()
        minb = b.min()
        gap = maxb - minb
        if gap <= 1e-18:
            break
        # active
        threshold = maxb - 0.5 * gap
        active = np.flatnonzero(b >= threshold)
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
        elif active.size > max_active_cur:
            # top max_active_cur
            active = np.argpartition(b, -max_active_cur)[-max_active_cur:]
        active_b = b[active]
        temp = max(1e-18, temp_factor_cur * gap)
        w = np.exp((active_b - maxb) / temp)
        sw = w.sum()
        if sw <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones_like(active_b) / active_b.size
        else:
            w = w / sw
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wi, k in zip(w, active):
            kint = int(k)
            lo = kint - n + 1
            if lo < 0: lo = 0
            hi = kint
            if hi >= n: hi = n - 1
            if lo <= hi:
                idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                grad[idx] += (2.0 * wi) * y[kint - idx]
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            # random small perturbation
            a = a * (1.0 + 1e-5*rng.normal(size=n))
            a=normalize(a); a_m=fft_conv(a)[1]; prev=a.copy(); continue
        eta = step / gmax
        if eta > 1e5: eta = 1e5
        logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
        v = logy - eta * grad
        vmax = np.max(v)
        new = np.exp(v - vmax)
        snew = new.sum()
        if snew <= 1e-300:
            new = y.copy()
        else:
            new = new / snew
        # ensure finite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new)):
            new = y.copy()
        new_b, new_m = fft_conv(new)
        # accept criteria
        accepted = False
        if new_m < a_m - 1e-15:
            a = new; prev = a.copy()? Wait prev for next Nesterov should be old a? In Nesterov, y uses new? Standard:
            # x_{t+1} from x_t, y_t; set prev = x_t (old), x = x_{t+1}
```
We need maintain `prev` correctly. Let's define variables: at loop start, `a` current x_t, `prev` x_{t-1}. y = a + mu*(a - prev). If accept new x_{t+1}, for next iteration prev should be old a (x_t), a should new. If reject, keep a and set prev to a (so next momentum zero). So:
```
old_a = a
if accept:
   a = new
   prev = old_a.copy()
   a_m = new_m
else:
   prev = old_a.copy()
```
But if we set prev old, next y = a + mu*(a - prev) = new + mu*(new-old) okay. For reject, prev=a -> y=a. Good. Use `old=a` before acceptance.

For anneal accept uphill (new_m < a_m + accept_temp), set a=new, prev old. If new_m > best_m, best unchanged, stall increments. For downhill, best update.
```
if new_m < a_m - 1e-15:
   accept; if new_m < best_m - tol: update best, stall=0 else stall=1; step=min(step*1.15,0.6)
elif new_m < a_m + accept_temp:
   accept; if new_m < best_m: update; else stall+=1; step=min(step*1.05,0.5)
else:
   step=max(step*0.65,0.004); stall+=1
```
But if many uphill accepted, a_m can drift high. Stall counts best improvements. If stall > 2000, restart:
```
if stall > 2500:
   noise = 0.01 + 0.01*min(restarts,6)
   a_trial = best + rng.normal(0, noise*best)
   a_trial = normalize(a_trial)
   if fft_conv_max(a_trial) < a_m*1.2: accept as current a, a_m=..., prev=a.copy(); step=start_step; restarts++
   else: a=best.copy(); a_m=best_m; prev=a.copy(); step=...
   stall=0
```
Need if we restart, `prev` old.
Adapt temp factor and active:

```

```

The gradient formula for active weights: For b_k = ∑_{i} a_i a_{k-i}. Or compute inside. To avoid duplicating compute if skip, in exact_cd: compute start,end,out, then if not skip, call `compute_move_lin_quad`, then `finish_move_eval`.
```
def finish_move_eval(a,b,i,j,fracs,start,end,lin,quad,outside,current_max):
   base=b[start:end]; ai=a[i]; best_new=current_max; best_delta=0
   for fr in fracs:
       d=ai*fr
       if d<=0: continue
       # maybe if d > ai: d=ai
       new = base + lin*d + quad*d*d
       mx=float(new.max())
       if mx < outside: mx = outside
       if mx < best_new:
          best_new=mx; best_delta=d
   return best_delta,best_new
```

Let's write exact_cd loop with no nested functions; use for loops.

Pseudo exact_cd full:
```
def exact_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0, max_candidates=24, top_peaks=80, verbose=False):
    t0=...
    a=normalize(a0); b, a_m=fft_conv(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=a_m
    cur_m=a_m
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall=0
    fracs=(0.002,0.008,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        # ensure cur_m current
        cur_m = float(b.max())
        minb=float(b.min()); gap=cur_m-minb
        if gap <= 1e-18: break
        pref=np.maximum.accumulate(b)
        suff=np.maximum.accumulate(b[::-1])[::-1]
        peak_count = max(20, min(top_peaks, L))
        if peak_count >= L:
           active=np.arange(L)
        else:
           active=np.argpartition(b, -peak_count)[-peak_count:]
        # weights
        temp=0.1*gap
        active_b=b[active]
        w=np.exp((active_b-cur_m)/temp)
        sw=w.sum()
        if sw<=0: w=np.ones_like(active_b)/len
        else: w/=sw
        score=np.zeros(n)
        for wi,k in zip(w,active):
            ki=int(k); lo=ki-n+1; if lo<0: lo=0; hi=ki; if hi>=n: hi=n-1
            idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
            score[idx] += wi * a[idx] * a[ki-idx]
        src_score=score*a
        raw_cand=[]
        # top sources 16, low dest 12
        top_src_count = min(16, n)
        top_src = np.argpartition(-src_score, top_src_count)[:top_src_count]
        low_dest_count = min(32, n)
        low_dest = np.argpartition(score, low_dest_count)[:low_dest_count]
        for i in top_src:
           i=int(i)
           if a[i] <= 1e-12: continue
           for j in low_dest[:10]:
              j=int(j)
              if j != i: raw_cand.append((i,j))
        # low a dest
        low_a_count = min(10,n)
        low_a = np.argpartition(a, low_a_count)[:low_a_count]
        for i in top_src[:8]:
           i=int(i)
           if a[i] <= 1e-12: continue
           for j in low_a:
              j=int(j)
              if j != i and a[j] < 0.5*a[i]? maybe all
                 raw_cand.append((i,j))
        # active peak specific
        for k in active[:10]:
           ki=int(k); lo...; idx=np.arange(...)
           prod=a[idx]*a[ki-idx]
           imax=int(np.argmax(prod))
           src=int(idx[imax])
           # choose dest low_dest not src
           for j in low_dest[:12]:
              j=int(j)
              if j != src:
                 raw_cand.append((src,j)); break
        # random 10
        if n>1:
           for _ in range(10):
              i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
              if i != j: raw_cand.append((i,j))
        # dedup
        cand=[]; seen=set()
        for pair in raw_cand:
           key=(pair[0],pair[1])
           if key not in seen and pair[0]!=pair[1]:
               seen.add(key); cand.append(key)
        if not cand:
           stall += 1
        else:
           if len(cand) > max_candidates:
              perm=rng.permutation(len(cand))[0:max_candidates]
           else:
              perm=rng.permutation(len(cand))
           improved=False
           for pi in perm:
              i,j=cand[int(pi)]
              ai=a[i]
              if ai <= 1e-12: continue
              start = min(i,j); end = max(i,j)+n
              out=0.0
              if start>0: out=pref[start-1]
              if end<L:
                 v=suff[end]; if v>out: out=v
              if out >= cur_m - 1e-15: continue
              sq = compute_move_lin_quad(a,i,j)
              # sq returns start,end,lin,quad
              # (should match start,end)
              d,newm = finish_move_eval(a,b,i,ai,fracs,sq[0],sq[1],sq[2],sq[3],out,cur_m)
              if d > 0.0 and newm < cur_m - 1e-15:
                  apply_move(a,b,i,j,d,sq[0],sq[1],sq[2],sq[3])
                  cur_m = newm
                  if cur_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                     best = a.copy(); best_m = cur_m
                  stall=0
                  improved=True
                  break
           if improved:
              continue
           # random downhill polish a bit
           polished=False
           # fixed tries based on n? 200
           for _ in range(300):
              if time... break
              i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n));
              if i==j: continue
              ai=a[i]; if ai<=1e-12 continue
              start=... out...
              if out>=cur_m-1e-15: continue
              sq=compute_move_lin_quad(...)
              # use smaller fracs after? all
              d,newm=finish_move_eval(...)
              if d>0 and newm<cur_m-1e-15:
                 apply; update; polished=True; break
           if polished:
              stall=0
              continue

def finish_move_eval(ai, b, start, end, lin, quad, fracs, outside, current_max):
    base=b[start:end]
    best_new=current_max; best_d=0.0
    for fr in fracs:
       d=ai*fr
       if d <= 0: continue
       new=base + lin*d + quad*d*d
       mx=float(new.max())
       if mx < outside: mx=outside
       if mx < best_new:
           best_new=mx; best_d=d
    return best_d,best_new
```

```
def make_lowfreq(n, seed=0, max_m=10, amp=0.6):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    v = 0.4 * (rng.normal(0, 1.0) * x)  # maybe monotone
    for m in range(1, max_m+1):
        c = rng.normal(0.0, amp / np.sqrt(float(m)))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, TWO_PI)
        v = v + c * np.cos(TWO_PI * m * x + ph)
    f = 1.0 + v
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-3)
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.ones(n)/n
    return f/s
```
If n=1, linspace [0], okay.

`make_beta_like`:
```
def make_beta_like(n,p=0.5,q=1.0):
   if n<=1: return ones
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # avoid 0**negative? p positive. If p<0, inf. Use x+eps,1-x+eps.
   eps=1e-6
   f=np.power(x+eps, p) * np.power(1.0-x+eps, q)
   f=np.maximum(f,0)
   return normalize(f)
```
p,q nonnegative.

`interpolate_seq`:
```
def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
   a=normalize(a); old_n=len(a)
   if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
   old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
   new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
   b=np.maximum(b,0)
   s=b.sum()
   if s<=1e-12: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
   return b/s
```
If old_n=1, old_x [0], interp returns constant a[0]. Good.

`remaining_time(t0,T)`:
```
def remaining_time(t0, T):
    dt=time.monotonic()-t0
    rem=T-dt
    return rem if rem>0 else 0.0
```

def fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s < 1e-12 or a.size==0: return inf
   _,M=fft_conv(a)
   sc=2.0*len(a)*M/(s*s)
   return sc if finite else inf
```

def clean_ns(lst, lo, hi, limit=8):
    out=[]; seen=set()
    for x in lst:
       try: xi=int(x)
       except: continue
       if xi>=lo and xi<=hi and xi not in seen:
          out.append(xi); seen.add(xi)
          if len(out)>=limit: break
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The search combines:
  1. A normalized mirror/softmax accelerated descent directly on the peak
     convolution, with adaptive step size and activity weighting.
  2. Directed exact pair-move local search.  Moves are evaluated by updating
     only the affected convolution interval, giving cheap exact changes.
  3. Multi-length / multi-initialization exploration using smooth low-frequency
     densities and beta-like skewed shapes.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

TWO_PI = 2.0 * np.pi

MAX_N_MAIN = 14000
MAX_N_CD = 10000


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    if n == 1:
        b = np.array([float(a[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
        return b, float(b[0])

    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 256:
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        return b, float(np.max(b))

    size = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=size)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=size)
    np.maximum(B, 0.0, out=B)
    b = B[:L]
    return b, float(np.max(b))


def fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    _, M = fft_conv(a)
    sc = 2.0 * float(a.size) * M / (s * s)
    return float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else float("inf")


def official_score(a):
    try:
        vals = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)]
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def remaining_time(t0, total_budget):
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    rem = float(total_budget) - dt
    return rem if rem > 0.0 else 0.0


def clean_ns(lst, lo, hi, limit=8):
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for x in lst:
        try:
            xi = int(x)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if xi >= lo and xi <= hi and xi not in seen:
            out.append(xi)
            seen.add(xi)
            if len(out) >= limit:
                break
    return out


def indices_top(arr, k, smallest=False):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(arr.size)
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if n <= k:
        return np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    if smallest:
        return np.argpartition(arr, k - 1)[:k]
    return np.argpartition(arr, -k)[-k:]


def make_lowfreq(n, seed=0, max_m=10, amp=0.6):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    v = 0.35 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * x
    for m in range(1, int(max_m) + 1):
        c = rng.normal(0.0, float(amp) / np.sqrt(float(m)))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, TWO_PI)
        v = v + c * np.cos(TWO_PI * float(m) * x + ph)
    f = 1.0 + v
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-3)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return f / s


def make_beta_like(n, p=0.5, q=1.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = 1e-6
    f = np.power(x + eps, float(p)) * np.power(1.0 - x + eps, float(q))
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    return normalize(f)


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    a = normalize(a)
    old_n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return b / s


def update_best(tracker, a, label):
    if a is None:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return
    sc = fast_score(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if tracker["seq"] is None or sc < tracker["score"] - 1e-13:
        arr = arr / s
        tracker["seq"] = arr
        tracker["score"] = float(sc)
        print(f"  NEW BEST {label}: fast={sc:.7f} n={len(arr)}")


def mirror_search(a0, time_limit, seed=0, start_step=0.08, max_step=0.6,
                  max_active=80, temp_factor=0.30, acceptance_rel=1e-4,
                  verbose=False):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return normalize(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    b, a_m = fft_conv(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = float(a_m)

    prev = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    step = float(start_step)
    max_active_cur = max(8, min(int(max_active), 2 * n - 1))
    tf = max(0.02, float(temp_factor))
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0

    while remaining_time(t0, time_limit) > 0.0:
        it += 1

        if it == 1:
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            if it < 50:
                mu = 0.5
            elif it < 500:
                mu = 0.8
            else:
                mu = 0.9
            y = a + mu * (a - prev)
            ymin = float(np.min(y))
            if ymin < 0.0:
                y = y + (-ymin + 1e-15)
            sy = float(y.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sy) or sy <= 1e-15:
                y = a.copy()
            else:
                y = y / sy

        b, y_m = fft_conv(y)
        maxb = float(y_m)
        minb = float(np.min(b))
        gap = maxb - minb
        if gap <= 1e-18:
            break

        threshold = maxb - max(1e-18, 0.5 * gap)
        active = np.flatnonzero(b >= threshold)
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
        elif active.size > max_active_cur:
            active = indices_top(b, max_active_cur, smallest=False)

        active_b = b[active]
        temp = max(1e-18, tf * gap)
        w = np.exp((active_b - maxb) / temp)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones_like(active_b, dtype=np.float64) / float(active_b.size)
        else:
            w = w / sw

        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wi, k in zip(w, active):
            kint = int(k)
            lo = kint - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = kint
            if hi >= n:
                hi = n - 1
            if lo <= hi:
                idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                grad[idx] += (2.0 * float(wi)) * y[kint - idx]

        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if n > 0 else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            a = normalize(a * (1.0 + 1e-5 * rng.normal(size=n)))
            a_m = float(fft_conv(a)[1])
            prev = a.copy()
            continue

        eta = step / gmax
        if eta > 1.0e5:
            eta = 1.0e5
        logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
        v = logy - eta * grad
        vmax = float(np.max(v))
        new = np.exp(v - vmax)
        snew = float(new.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 1e-300:
            new = y.copy()
        else:
            new = new / snew

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(new)):
            new = y.copy()

        new_b, new_m = fft_conv(new)
        new_m = float(new_m)

        old_a = a
        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(new_m) and new_m < a_m - 1e-15:
            accept = True
            step = min(max_step, step * 1.12)
        elif np.isfinite(new_m) and np.isfinite(best_m):
            accept_temp = acceptance_rel * max(best_m, 1e-300) * max(0.1, np.exp(-it / 8000.0))
            if new_m < a_m + accept_temp:
                accept = True
                step = min(max_step, step * 1.02)

        if accept:
            a = new
            prev = old_a.copy()
            a_m = new_m
            if a_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = a_m
                best = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                if verbose and (it % 1000 == 0):
                    print(f"    mirror improve it={it} M={best_m:.10f}")
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            prev = old_a.copy()
            step = max(0.003, step * 0.65)
            stall += 1

        if it % 100 == 0:
            tf = max(0.02, tf * 0.985)
            max_active_cur = min(int(max_active), max_active_cur + 2)

        if stall > 2500:
            restarts += 1
            scale = 0.005 + 0.004 * float(min(restarts, 12))
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
            a_try = best * np.exp(noise)
            a_try = normalize(a_try)
            a_try_m = float(fft_conv(a_try)[1])
            if np.isfinite(a_try_m):
                a = a_try
                a_m = a_try_m
            else:
                a = best.copy()
                a_m = best_m
            prev = a.copy()
            step = min(max_step, float(start_step) * (1.0 + 0.25 * min(restarts, 6)))
            tf = max(float(temp_factor), tf * 1.6)
            max_active_cur = max(8, min(int(max_active), max_active_cur // 2))
            stall = 0

    best = normalize(best)
    return best


def compute_move_lin_quad(a, i, j):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    start = int(min(i, j))
    end = int(max(i, j)) + n
    if end > L:
        end = L
    length = end - start
    lin = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
    quad = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)

    ai = float(a[i])
    sj = float(a[j])

    s_start = max(start, int(i))
    s_end = min(end, int(i) + n)
    if s_start < s_end:
        ks = np.arange(s_start, s_end, dtype=np.int64)
        mirror = ks - int(i)
        lin[ks - start] -= 2.0 * ai * a[mirror]
        k_self = 2 * int(i)
        if s_start <= k_self < s_end:
            quad[k_self - start] += 1.0

    d_start = max(start, int(j))
    d_end = min(end, int(j) + n)
    if d_start < d_end:
        ks = np.arange(d_start, d_end, dtype=np.int64)
        mirror = ks - int(j)
        lin[ks - start] += 2.0 * sj * a[mirror]
        k_self = 2 * int(j)
        if d_start <= k_self < d_end:
            quad[k_self - start] += 1.0

    if int(i) != int(j):
        k_ij = int(i) + int(j)
        if start <= k_ij < end:
            quad[k_ij - start] -= 2.0

    return start, end, lin, quad


def finish_move_eval(ai, b, start, end, lin, quad, fracs, outside, current_max):
    base = b[start:end]
    best_d = 0.0
    best_new = float(current_max)
    for f in fracs:
        d = float(ai) * float(f)
        if d <= 0.0:
            continue
        new_vals = base + lin * d + quad * (d * d)
        mx = float(np.max(new_vals))
        if mx < float(outside):
            mx = float(outside)
        if mx < best_new:
            best_new = mx
            best_d = d
    return best_d, best_new


def apply_move(a, b, i, j, delta, start, end, lin, quad):
    if delta <= 0.0:
        return
    a[i] -= delta
    a[j] += delta
    b[start:end] = b[start:end] + lin * delta + quad * (delta * delta)
    seg = b[start:end]
    if np.any(seg < 0.0):
        np.maximum(seg, 0.0, out=seg)


def exact_cd(a0, time_limit, seed=0, max_candidates=24, top_peaks=80,
             verbose=False):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size < 2:
        return normalize(a0)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return normalize(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize(a0)
    b, cur_m = fft_conv(a)
    cur_m = float(cur_m)
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = cur_m
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    fracs = (0.002, 0.008, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80)
    stall = 0
    move_count = 0

    while remaining_time(t0, time_limit) > 0.0:
        cur_m = float(np.max(b))
        minb = float(np.min(b))
        gap = cur_m - minb
        if gap <= 1e-18:
            break

        pref = np.maximum.accumulate(b)
        rev = np.maximum.accumulate(b[::-1])
        suff = rev[::-1]

        peak_count = max(20, min(int(top_peaks), L))
        if peak_count >= L:
            active = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            active = indices_top(b, peak_count, smallest=False)
        if active.size > 0:
            active = active[np.argsort(b[active])[::-1][:peak_count]]

        temp = max(1e-18, 0.1 * gap)
        active_b = b[active]
        w = np.exp((active_b - cur_m) / temp)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw <= 1e-300:
            w = np.ones_like(active_b, dtype=np.float64) / float(active_b.size)
        else:
            w = w / sw

        score = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for wi, k in zip(w, active):
            kint = int(k)
            lo = kint - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = kint
            if hi >= n:
                hi = n - 1
            if lo <= hi:
                idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                score[idx] += float(wi) * a[idx] * a[kint - idx]

        src_score = score * a
        raw_cand = []

        top_src_count = min(16, n)
        top_src = indices_top(src_score, top_src_count, smallest=False)
        low_dest = indices_top(score, min(32, n), smallest=True)

        for ii in top_src:
            i = int(ii)
            if float(a[i]) <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for jj in low_dest[:10]:
                j = int(jj)
                if j != i:
                    raw_cand.append((i, j))

        low_a = indices_top(a, min(10, n), smallest=True)
        for ii in top_src[:8]:
            i = int(ii)
            if float(a[i]) <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for jj in low_a[:10]:
                j = int(jj)
                if j != i:
                    raw_cand.append((i, j))

        for k in active[:10]:
            kint = int(k)
            lo = kint - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = kint
            if hi >= n:
                hi = n - 1
            if lo > hi:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            prod = a[idx] * a[kint - idx]
            imx = int(np.argmax(prod))
            src = int(idx[imx])
            for jj in low_dest[:12]:
                j = int(jj)
                if j != src:
                    raw_cand.append((src, j))
                    break

        for _ in range(10):
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i != j:
                raw_cand.append((i, j))

        cand = []
        seen = set()
        for while_i, while_j in raw_cand:
            key = (int(while_i), int(while_j))
            if key[0] != key[1] and key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                cand.append(key)

        improved = False
        if cand:
            if len(cand) > int(max_candidates):
                perm = rng.permutation(len(cand))[:max_candidates]
            else:
                perm = rng.permutation(len(cand))
            for pi in perm:
                if remaining_time(t0, time_limit) <= 0.0:
                    break
                i, j = cand[int(pi)]
                ai = float(a[i])
                if ai <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                start = int(min(i, j))
                end = int(max(i, j)) + n
                if end > L:
                    end = L
                out = 0.0
                if start > 0:
                    out = float(pref[start - 1])
                if end < L:
                    val = float(suff[end])
                    if val > out:
                        out = val
                if out >= cur_m - 1e-15:
                    continue
                mstart, mend, lin, quad = compute_move_lin_quad(a, i, j)
                bd, newm = finish_move_eval(ai, b, mstart, mend, lin, quad,
                                            fracs, out, cur_m)
                if bd > 0.0 and newm < cur_m - 1e-15:
                    apply_move(a, b, i, j, bd, mstart, mend, lin, quad)
                    cur_m = float(newm)
                    move_count += 1
                    if cur_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                        best_m = cur_m
                        best = a.copy()
                    if move_count % 100 == 0:
                        b_new, cm_new = fft_conv(a)
                        b = b_new
                        cur_m = float(cm_new)
                        if cur_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                            best_m = cur_m
                            best = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            continue

        polished = False
        for _ in range(220):
            if remaining_time(t0, time_limit) <= 0.0:
                break
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int(rng.integers(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            ai = float(a[i])
            if ai <= 1e-12:
                continue
            start = int(min(i, j))
            end = int(max(i, j)) + n
            if end > L:
                end = L
            out = 0.0
            if start > 0:
                out = float(pref[start - 1])
            if end < L:
                val = float(suff[end])
                if val > out:
                    out = val
            if out >= cur_m - 1e-15:
                continue
            mstart, mend, lin, quad = compute_move_lin_quad(a, i, j)
            bd, newm = finish_move_eval(ai, b, mstart, mend, lin, quad,
                                        fracs, out, cur_m)
            if bd > 0.0 and newm < cur_m - 1e-15:
                apply_move(a, b, i, j, bd, mstart, mend, lin, quad)
                cur_m = float(newm)
                move_count += 1
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best = a.copy()
                polished = True
                break

        if polished:
            stall = 0
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall > 150:
            break

    best = normalize(best)
    return best, best_m


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    T = min(max(float(budget_s) - 60.0, 120.0), 1740.0)

    tracker = {
        "seq": None,
        "score": float("inf"),
    }

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 10 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum() > 1e-12:
            parent = p.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
            if parent.size > MAX_N_MAIN:
                parent = interpolate_seq(parent, MAX_N_MAIN)
            parent = normalize(parent)
            sc = fast_score(parent)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                tracker["seq"] = parent.copy()
                tracker["score"] = float(sc)
                print(f"Parent loaded: n={len(parent)}, fast={sc:.7f}")
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if tracker["seq"] is None:
        n0 = 4700
        best_a = None
        best_sc = float("inf")
        for sd in range(6):
            a_try = make_lowfreq(n0, seed=123 + sd * 17, max_m=10 + (sd % 2) * 2,
                                 amp=0.65)
            sc_try = fast_score(a_try)
            if sc_try < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_try
                best_a = a_try
        for p, q in [(0.30, 1.20), (1.20, 0.30), (0.60, 0.60),
                     (0.20, 2.00), (2.00, 0.20), (1.00, 0.40)]:
            a_try = make_beta_like(n0, p, q)
            sc_try = fast_score(a_try)
            if sc_try < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_try
                best_a = a_try
        if best_a is None:
            best_a = make_lowfreq(n0, 1, 12)
        tracker["seq"] = normalize(best_a)
        tracker["score"] = fast_score(tracker["seq"])
        print(f"No parent; initialized n={len(tracker['seq'])}, fast={tracker['score']:.7f}")

    # Phase 1: main accelerated mirror search on current best length.
    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    t1 = min(rem * 0.36, 620.0)
    if t1 > 30.0:
        a_out = mirror_search(tracker["seq"], t1, seed=101,
                              start_step=0.075, max_step=0.6,
                              max_active=120, temp_factor=0.35)
        update_best(tracker, a_out, "mirror1")

    # Phase 2: exact directed pair-move polish.
    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    n_cur = len(tracker["seq"])
    if n_cur <= MAX_N_CD:
        t2 = min(rem * 0.12, 180.0)
        if t2 > 20.0:
            a_out, _ = exact_cd(tracker["seq"], t2, seed=202,
                                max_candidates=26, top_peaks=90)
            update_best(tracker, a_out, "cd1")

    # Phase 3: tight mirror refinement.
    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    t3 = min(rem * 0.14, 210.0)
    if t3 > 20.0:
        a_out = mirror_search(tracker["seq"], t3, seed=303,
                              start_step=0.045, max_step=0.35,
                              max_active=160, temp_factor=0.12)
        update_best(tracker, a_out, "mirror2")

    # Phase 4: length and initialization exploration.
    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    expl_budget = min(rem * 0.34, 560.0)
    if expl_budget > 70.0:
        n_cur = len(tracker["seq"])
        raw_ns = [
            n_cur,
            n_cur + 1000,
            n_cur - 1000,
            n_cur + 2000,
            n_cur - 2000,
            n_cur + 4000,
            3000,
            5000,
            7000,
            9000,
            11000,
        ]
        ns = clean_ns(raw_ns, 3000, MAX_N_MAIN, 6)

        for idx_n, new_n in enumerate(ns):
            if remaining_time(t_global, T) < 90.0:
                break
            print(f"\nExplores length n={new_n}")

            a_int = interpolate_seq(tracker["seq"], new_n)
            update_best(tracker, a_int, f"interp{new_n}")

            tA = min(remaining_time(t_global, T) - 80.0, 45.0)
            if tA > 10.0:
                a_out = mirror_search(a_int, tA, seed=404 + idx_n * 17,
                                      start_step=0.08, max_step=0.55,
                                      max_active=90, temp_factor=0.32)
                update_best(tracker, a_out, f"amd{new_n}")
                if new_n <= MAX_N_CD:
                    tC = min(remaining_time(t_global, T) - 60.0, 12.0)
                    if tC > 5.0:
                        a_out2, _ = exact_cd(a_out, tC, seed=505 + idx_n * 19,
                                             max_candidates=16, top_peaks=60)
                        update_best(tracker, a_out2, f"cd{new_n}")

            if remaining_time(t_global, T) > 60.0:
                a_fresh = make_lowfreq(new_n, seed=910 + idx_n * 31, max_m=12,
                                       amp=0.7)
                tB = min(remaining_time(t_global, T) - 60.0, 35.0)
                if tB > 8.0:
                    a_out = mirror_search(a_fresh, tB, seed=961 + idx_n * 37,
                                          start_step=0.08, max_step=0.55,
                                          max_active=90, temp_factor=0.38)
                    update_best(tracker, a_out, f"fresh{new_n}")
                if new_n <= MAX_N_CD and remaining_time(t_global, T) > 45.0:
                    tD = min(remaining_time(t_global, T) - 45.0, 8.0)
                    if tD > 4.0:
                        a_out2, _ = exact_cd(a_out, tD, seed=991 + idx_n * 41,
                                             max_candidates=12, top_peaks=50)
                        update_best(tracker, a_out2, f"freshcd{new_n}")

    # Phase 5: final long polish.
    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    t5 = min(rem * 0.58, 320.0)
    if t5 > 25.0:
        a_out = mirror_search(tracker["seq"], t5, seed=1234,
                              start_step=0.035, max_step=0.30,
                              max_active=180, temp_factor=0.08)
        update_best(tracker, a_out, "mirror_final")

    rem = remaining_time(t_global, T)
    n_cur = len(tracker["seq"])
    if n_cur <= MAX_N_CD and rem > 35.0:
        t6 = min(rem * 0.65, 260.0)
        if t6 > 15.0:
            a_out, _ = exact_cd(tracker["seq"], t6, seed=5678,
                                max_candidates=32, top_peaks=130)
            update_best(tracker, a_out, "cd_final")

    seq = tracker["seq"]
    if seq is None or seq.size == 0:
        seq = make_lowfreq(4700, 4242, 12)

    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    seq = normalize(seq)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(seq)):
        seq = make_lowfreq(4700, 777, 12)

    final_fast = fast_score(seq)
    final_official = official_score(seq)
    print(f"FINAL: n={len(seq)}, fast={final_fast:.7f}, official={final_official:.7f}, "
          f"time={time.monotonic() - t_global:.1f}s")

    return [float(v) for v in seq]
```
